Rights basis: published 1699, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: legible but rough Portuguese: the two columns of each page run into one stream, the long s is read as f, accents are garbled and marginal citations are mixed in. Content note: Half-title, 'Tomo XII', at OCR lines 12-20; title page at 23-53, dedicated on its face to the Immaculate Conception. Licences at 67-518, dated from Bahia, 20 June 1697, to Lisbon, 1698; the list of the sixteen sermons at 519-558. Sermon I, of the Conception, at 563; II, of St. Roch, 2149; III 4695; IV 6553; V, of St. Anthony, 8840; VI 10902; VII 12073; VIII 13779; IX 16443; X 18492; XI, of St. Anthony, preached at Rome, 20286; XII 23703; XIII, preached in Maranhão, 25344; XIV 27271; XV, of St. Joseph, 28897; XVI 30360.
